# FILE 8 / 146
# PATH : docker-compose.yml
# SIZE : 3045 B
# EXT  : .yml
####################################################################################################
services:
  postgres:
    image: pgvector/pgvector:0.8.6-pg16
    container_name: mcp-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB}
    ports:
      - "${POSTGRES_PORT}:5432"
    volumes:
      - mcp-postgres-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
      - ./docker/init-db.sql:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/init-db.sql:ro
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U \"$${POSTGRES_USER}\" -d \"$${POSTGRES_DB}\""]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

  taskservice:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: src/Services/TaskService.Api/Dockerfile
    container_name: mcp-taskservice
    environment:
      ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT: ${ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT}
      ConnectionStrings__taskdb: "Host=postgres;Database=${TASK_DB};Username=${POSTGRES_USER};Password=${POSTGRES_PASSWORD};GSS Encryption Mode=Disable"
    ports:
      - "${TASKSERVICE_PORT}:8080"
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy

  documentservice:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: src/Services/DocumentService.Api/Dockerfile
    container_name: mcp-documentservice
    environment:
      ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT: ${ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT}
      ConnectionStrings__documentdb: "Host=postgres;Database=${DOCUMENT_DB};Username=${POSTGRES_USER};Password=${POSTGRES_PASSWORD};GSS Encryption Mode=Disable"
    ports:
      - "${DOCUMENTSERVICE_PORT}:8080"
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy

  gateway:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: src/Gateway/Gateway.Api/Dockerfile
    container_name: mcp-gateway
    environment:
      ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT: ${ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT}

      OAuth__Authority: ${OAUTH_AUTHORITY}
      OAuth__Audience: ${OAUTH_AUDIENCE}

      Jwt__Key: ${JWT_KEY}
      Jwt__Issuer: ${JWT_ISSUER}
      Jwt__Audience: ${JWT_AUDIENCE}

      ConnectionStrings__userdb: "Host=postgres;Database=${USER_DB};Username=${POSTGRES_USER};Password=${POSTGRES_PASSWORD};GSS Encryption Mode=Disable"

      services__taskservice__http__0: "http://taskservice:8080"
      services__documentservice__http__0: "http://documentservice:8080"
    volumes:
      - gateway-keys:/app/keys
    ports:
      - "${GATEWAY_PORT}:8080"
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
      taskservice:
        condition: service_started
      documentservice:
        condition: service_started

  agent:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: src/Agent/Agent.Api/Dockerfile
    container_name: mcp-agent
    environment:
      ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT: ${ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT}
      Ollama__BaseUrl: ${OLLAMA_BASE_URL}
      Ollama__Model: ${OLLAMA_MODEL}
      Gateway__McpUrl: ${AGENT_GATEWAY_URL}
    ports:
      - "${AGENT_PORT}:8080"
    depends_on:
      gateway:
        condition: service_started

volumes:
  mcp-postgres-data:
  gateway-keys:

# FILE 21 / 146
# PATH : src\Agent\Agent.Api\Program.cs
# SIZE : 2378 B
# EXT  : .cs
####################################################################################################
using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
using System.Security.Claims;

using Agent.Api.Contracts;
using Agent.Api.Services;
using Agent.Services;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.Services.AddSingleton<IAgentService, AgentService>();

builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
{
    options.AddPolicy("Client", policy =>
    {
        policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
            .AllowAnyHeader()
            .AllowAnyMethod()
            .AllowCredentials();
    });
});

var app = builder.Build();

app.UseCors("Client");

app.MapPost("/api/agent/chat", async (
    ChatRequest request,
    HttpContext context,
    IAgentService agent,
    CancellationToken cancellationToken) =>
{
    var authHeader = context.Request.Headers["Authorization"].ToString();
    var cookieToken = context.Request.Cookies["access_token"];

    Console.WriteLine($"[AGENT] AuthHeader present: {!string.IsNullOrEmpty(authHeader)}");
    Console.WriteLine($"[AGENT] Cookie present: {cookieToken != null}");

    var jwt = authHeader.Replace("Bearer ", "").Trim();
    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(jwt))
        jwt = cookieToken ?? string.Empty;

    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(jwt))
    {
        Console.WriteLine("[AGENT] No JWT found");
        return Results.Unauthorized();
    }

    try
    {
        var handler = new JwtSecurityTokenHandler();
        var token = handler.ReadJwtToken(jwt);

        Console.WriteLine("[AGENT] === JWT Claims ===");
        foreach (var claim in token.Claims)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[AGENT] {claim.Type} = {claim.Value}");
        }
        Console.WriteLine("[AGENT] ===================");

        var subjectId = token.Claims.FirstOrDefault(c =>
            c.Type == "nameid" ||
            c.Type == "sub" ||
            c.Type == ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier ||
            c.Type.EndsWith("nameidentifier", StringComparison.Ordinal))?.Value;

        Console.WriteLine($"[AGENT] JWT subjectId = {subjectId ?? "(null)"}");
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"[AGENT] JWT parse error: {ex.Message}");
    }

    var (reply, toolsUsed) = await agent.ChatAsync(
        request.SessionId, request.Message, jwt, cancellationToken);

    return Results.Ok(new ChatResponse(reply, toolsUsed));
});

app.MapGet("/health", () => Results.Ok(new { status = "healthy" }));

app.Run();

####################################################################################################
# FILE 23 / 146
# PATH : src\Agent\Agent.Api\Services\AgentService.cs
# SIZE : 9846 B
# EXT  : .cs
####################################################################################################
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Text.Json;

using Microsoft.Extensions.AI;

using OpenAI;

using Agent.Api.Services;
using ModelContextProtocol.Client;
using ModelContextProtocol.Protocol;

namespace Agent.Services;

public sealed class AgentService(
    IConfiguration configuration,
    ILogger<AgentService> logger) : IAgentService
{
    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, List<ChatMessage>> _sessions = new();

    private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, int, Exception?> _logSessionActivity =
        LoggerMessage.Define<string, int>(
            LogLevel.Information,
            new EventId(1, "SessionActivity"),
            "Session {SessionId}: {MessageCount} messages");

    private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, int, Exception?> _logToolsDiscovered =
        LoggerMessage.Define<string, int>(
            LogLevel.Information,
            new EventId(2, "ToolsDiscovered"),
            "Session {SessionId}: discovered {ToolCount} tools");

    public async Task<(string Reply, List<string> ToolsUsed)> ChatAsync(
        string sessionId, string message, string jwt, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var ollamaUrl = configuration["Ollama:BaseUrl"] ?? "http://localhost:11434";
        var model = configuration["Ollama:Model"] ?? "llama3.2";
        var gatewayMcpUrl = configuration["Gateway:McpUrl"] ?? "http://localhost:5000/mcp";

        var transport = new HttpClientTransport(new HttpClientTransportOptions
        {
            Endpoint = new Uri(gatewayMcpUrl),
            AdditionalHeaders = new Dictionary<string, string>
            {
                ["Authorization"] = $"Bearer {jwt}"
            }
        });

        await using var mcpClient = await McpClient.CreateAsync(transport, cancellationToken: cancellationToken);
        var tools = await mcpClient.ListToolsAsync(cancellationToken: cancellationToken);

        _logToolsDiscovered(logger, sessionId, tools.Count, null);

        var openAiClient = new OpenAIClient(
            new System.ClientModel.ApiKeyCredential("ollama"),
            new OpenAIClientOptions
            {
                Endpoint = new Uri($"{ollamaUrl.TrimEnd('/')}/v1")
            });

        IChatClient chatClient = openAiClient.GetChatClient(model).AsIChatClient();

        var toolsDescription = string.Join("\n", tools.Select(t =>
            $"- {t.Name}: {t.Description}"));

        var systemPrompt = $@"You are an AI assistant with access to these tools:
        {toolsDescription}

        RULES:
        1. When the user asks to create a task, respond with ONLY this JSON (no other text):
           {{""tool"": ""create_task"", ""arguments"": {{""title"": ""<title>"", ""description"": ""<description>""}}}}
           ALWAYS include both ""title"" and ""description"". If no description, use "".
        2. When the user asks to see tasks, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""list_tasks"", ""arguments"": {{}}}}
        3. When the user asks to complete a task, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""complete_task"", ""arguments"": {{""id"": 123}}}}
        4. When the user asks to search tasks, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""search_tasks"", ""arguments"": {{""keyword"": ""...""}}}}
        5. When the user asks to delete a task, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""delete_task"", ""arguments"": {{""id"": 123}}}}
        6. When the user asks to add a document, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""add_document"", ""arguments"": {{""title"": ""<title>"", ""content"": ""<content>""}}}}
           ALWAYS include both ""title"" and ""content"". If no content, use "".
        7. When the user asks to search documents, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""search_documents"", ""arguments"": {{""keyword"": ""...""}}}}
           ALWAYS include a non-empty ""keyword"". If the user didn't specify a keyword, respond with text asking for one.
        8. When the user asks to get a document by title, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""get_document"", ""arguments"": {{""title"": ""<title>""}}}}
           ALWAYS include the ""title"" field.
        9. When the user asks to delete a document, respond with ONLY:
           {{""tool"": ""delete_document"", ""arguments"": {{""title"": ""<title>""}}}}
           ALWAYS include the ""title"" field. Use the exact title from the user request.
        10. When the user asks to update a document, respond with ONLY:
            {{""tool"": ""update_document"", ""arguments"": {{""id"": 123, ""title"": ""<title>"", ""content"": ""<content>""}}}}
            ALWAYS include all three fields: ""id"", ""title"", and ""content"".
        11. NEVER invent tool names. Use ONLY these exact tools: {string.Join(", ", tools.Select(t => t.Name))}.
        12. If no tool matches the user request, respond with text explaining that you cannot help. DO NOT invent a tool.
        13. If the user asks for multiple actions, respond with MULTIPLE JSON objects, one per line.
        14. When the user asks to see ALL documents, respond with ONLY:
            {{""tool"": ""list_documents"", ""arguments"": {{}}}}
            Do NOT use search_documents when the user wants all documents.";

        var history = _sessions.GetOrAdd(sessionId, _ => new List<ChatMessage>
        {
            new(ChatRole.System, systemPrompt)
        });

        history.Add(new ChatMessage(ChatRole.User, message));

        var response = await chatClient.GetResponseAsync(
            history,
            new ChatOptions { ModelId = model },
            cancellationToken);

        var reply = response.Text;
        var toolsUsed = new List<string>();
        var results = new List<string>();

        var toolCalls = ParseAllToolCalls(reply);

        if (toolCalls.Count > 0)
        {
            foreach (var (toolName, arguments) in toolCalls)
            {
                var toolResult = await InvokeToolAsync(mcpClient, toolName, arguments, cancellationToken);
                toolsUsed.Add(toolName);
                results.Add(toolResult);
            }

            history.Add(new ChatMessage(ChatRole.Assistant, reply));
            history.Add(new ChatMessage(ChatRole.User, $"Tool results:\n{string.Join("\n", results)}"));

            var finalResponse = await chatClient.GetResponseAsync(
                history,
                new ChatOptions { ModelId = model },
                cancellationToken);

            var finalText = finalResponse.Text;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(finalText) ||
                finalText.TrimStart().StartsWith('{') ||
                finalText.TrimStart().StartsWith('['))
            {
                finalText = string.Join("\n", results);
            }
            else if (ParseAllToolCalls(finalText).Count > 0)
            {
                finalText = string.Join("\n", results);
            }

            _logSessionActivity(logger, sessionId, history.Count, null);
            return (finalText, toolsUsed);
        }

        _logSessionActivity(logger, sessionId, history.Count, null);
        return (reply, toolsUsed);
    }

    private static List<(string ToolName, Dictionary<string, object?> Arguments)> ParseAllToolCalls(string text)
    {
        var results = new List<(string, Dictionary<string, object?>)>();
        var index = 0;

        while (index < text.Length)
        {
            var start = text.IndexOf('{', index);
            if (start < 0) break;

            var depth = 0;
            var end = -1;
            for (int i = start; i < text.Length; i++)
            {
                if (text[i] == '{') depth++;
                else if (text[i] == '}')
                {
                    depth--;
                    if (depth == 0) { end = i; break; }
                }
            }
            if (end < 0) break;

            var json = text.Substring(start, end - start + 1);
            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                var root = doc.RootElement;

                if (root.TryGetProperty("tool", out var toolProp))
                {
                    var toolName = toolProp.GetString() ?? string.Empty;
                    var arguments = new Dictionary<string, object?>();

                    if (root.TryGetProperty("arguments", out var argsProp))
                    {
                        foreach (var prop in argsProp.EnumerateObject())
                        {
                            arguments[prop.Name] = prop.Value.ValueKind switch
                            {
                                JsonValueKind.String => prop.Value.GetString(),
                                JsonValueKind.Number => prop.Value.GetInt32(),
                                JsonValueKind.True => true,
                                JsonValueKind.False => false,
                                _ => null
                            };
                        }
                    }

                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(toolName))
                        results.Add((toolName, arguments));
                }
            }
            catch { }

            index = end + 1;
        }

        return results;
    }

    private static async Task<string> InvokeToolAsync(
        McpClient mcpClient, string toolName, Dictionary<string, object?> arguments, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var result = await mcpClient.CallToolAsync(toolName, arguments, cancellationToken: ct);
        return result.Content.OfType<TextContentBlock>().FirstOrDefault()?.Text ?? string.Empty;
    }
}

var builder = DistributedApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

var postgres = builder.AddPostgres("postgres")
    .WithDataVolume("mcp-postgres-data")
    .WithPgAdmin();

var taskDb = postgres.AddDatabase("taskdb");
var documentDb = postgres.AddDatabase("documentdb");
var userDb = postgres.AddDatabase("userdb");

var taskService = builder.AddProject<Projects.TaskService_Api>("taskservice")
    .WithReference(taskDb)
    .WithHttpEndpoint(port: 5001, name: "task-mcp");

var documentService = builder.AddProject<Projects.DocumentService_Api>("documentservice")
    .WithReference(documentDb)
    .WithHttpEndpoint(port: 5002, name: "document-mcp");

var gateway = builder.AddProject<Projects.Gateway_Api>("gateway")
    .WithReference(taskService)
    .WithReference(documentService)
    .WithReference(userDb)
    .WithHttpEndpoint(port: 5000, name: "gateway-mcp");

builder.AddViteApp("client", "../Web/Client")
    .WithReference(gateway)
    .WithHttpEndpoint(port: 5173, env: "PORT")
    .PublishAsDockerFile();

builder.AddProject<Projects.Agent_Api>("agent-api");

builder.Build().Run();

namespace Gateway.Api.Configuration
{
    using System.Text;

    using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
    using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;

    public sealed class JwtBearerOptionsSetup(IOptions<JwtOptions> jwtOptions)
        : IConfigureNamedOptions<JwtBearerOptions>
    {
        private readonly JwtOptions _options = jwtOptions.Value;

        public void Configure(JwtBearerOptions options)
        {
            Configure(JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme, options);
        }

        public void Configure(string? name, JwtBearerOptions options)
        {
            options.MapInboundClaims = false;

            options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
            {
                ValidateIssuer = true,
                ValidateAudience = true,
                ValidateLifetime = true,
                ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
                ValidIssuer = _options.Issuer,
                ValidAudience = _options.Audience,
                IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(_options.Key)),
                ClockSkew = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30)
            };

            options.Events = new JwtBearerEvents
            {
                OnMessageReceived = context =>
                {
                    if (context.Request.Cookies.TryGetValue("access_token", out var token))
                    {
                        context.Token = token;
                    }
                    return Task.CompletedTask;
                }
            };
        }
    }
}

namespace Gateway.Api.Configuration
{
    using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations; 

    public sealed class JwtOptions
    {
        public const string SectionName = "Jwt";

        [Required, MinLength(32)]
        public string Key { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        [Required]
        public string Issuer { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        [Required]
        public string Audience { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        [Range(1, 60)]
        public int AccessTokenMinutes { get; set; } = 15;
    }
}

namespace Gateway.Api.Data
{ 
    using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

    using Gateway.Core.Entities;

    public sealed class UserDbContext(DbContextOptions<UserDbContext> options) : DbContext(options)
    {
        public DbSet<User> Users => Set<User>();

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            modelBuilder.Entity<User>(entity =>
            {
                entity.HasKey(e => e.Id);
                entity.Property(e => e.Email).HasMaxLength(320).IsRequired();
                entity.HasIndex(e => e.Email).IsUnique();
                entity.Property(e => e.PasswordHash).IsRequired();
                entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt).IsRequired();
            });
        }
    }
}

COPY ["Directory.Packages.props", "./"]

RUN dotnet restore "./src/Gateway/Gateway.Api/Gateway.Api.csproj"
COPY . .
WORKDIR "/src/src/Gateway/Gateway.Api"
RUN dotnet build "./Gateway.Api.csproj" -c $BUILD_CONFIGURATION -o /app/build

FROM build AS publish

RUN dotnet publish "./Gateway.Api.csproj" -c $BUILD_CONFIGURATION -o /app/publish /p:UseAppHost=false

FROM base AS final
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=publish /app/publish .
ENTRYPOINT ["dotnet", "Gateway.Api.dll"]

namespace Gateway.Api.Endpoints
{
    using Gateway.Api.Data;
    using Gateway.Api.Infrastructure;
    using Gateway.Api.Services;
 
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Antiforgery;
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
    using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
    using System.Security.Claims;

    public static class AuthEndpoints
    {
        public static void MapAuthEndpoints(this WebApplication app)
        {
            var group = app.MapGroup("/api/auth").WithTags("Auth");

            group.MapPost("/register", Register)
                .AllowAnonymous()
                .WithName("Register");

            group.MapPost("/login", Login)
                .AllowAnonymous()
                .WithName("Login");

            group.MapPost("/logout", Logout)
                .RequireAuthorization()
                .WithName("Logout");

            group.MapGet("/me", Me)
                .RequireAuthorization()
                .WithName("Me");

            group.MapPost("/agent-token", (HttpContext context, IAuthService authService) =>
            {
                var subjectId = context.User.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)?.Value;
                var email = context.User.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.Email)?.Value;

                if (subjectId is null || email is null)
                    return Results.Unauthorized();

                var authUser = new AuthUserResponse(subjectId, email);
                var token = authService.GenerateTokenFor(authUser, "mcp.tools.read", "mcp.tools.write");
                return Results.Ok(new { accessToken = token });
            }).RequireAuthorization();
        }
         
        private static IResult Register(
            [FromBody] RegisterRequest request,
            [FromServices] IAuthService authService,
            [FromServices] ICookieWriter cookies,
            HttpContext context)
        {
            var user = authService.Register(request);
            if (user is null) return Results.BadRequest(new { error = "Invalid registration data" });

            var token = authService.GenerateTokenFor(user, "mcp.tools.read", "mcp.tools.write");
            if (token is null) return Results.Problem("Token generation failed");

            cookies.WriteAuthCookies(context, token);
            return Results.Ok(new LoginResponse(user));
        }

        private static IResult Login(
            [FromBody] LoginRequest request,
            [FromServices] IAuthService authService,
            [FromServices] ICookieWriter cookies,
            HttpContext context)
        {
            var user = authService.Login(request);
            if (user is null) return Results.Unauthorized();

            var token = authService.GenerateTokenFor(user, "mcp.tools.read", "mcp.tools.write");
            if (token is null) return Results.Problem("Token generation failed");

            cookies.WriteAuthCookies(context, token);
            return Results.Ok(new LoginResponse(user));
        }

        private static IResult AgentToken(
            string? email,
            [FromServices] UserDbContext db,
            [FromServices] IAuthService authService,
            [FromQuery] string? scopes)
        {
            var userEmail = email ?? "sekul.net@gmail.com";
            var user = db.Users.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefault(u => u.Email == userEmail);
            if (user is null) return Results.NotFound(new { error = "User not found" });

            var requestedScopes = string.IsNullOrEmpty(scopes)
                ? new[] { "mcp.tools.read", "mcp.tools.write" }
                : scopes.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);

            var authUser = new AuthUserResponse(user.Id.ToString(), user.Email);
            var token = authService.GenerateTokenFor(authUser, requestedScopes);
            return Results.Ok(new { accessToken = token });
        }

        private static async Task<IResult> Logout(
            ICookieWriter cookies,
            IAntiforgery antiforgery,
            HttpContext context)
        {
            await antiforgery.ValidateRequestAsync(context);
            cookies.ClearAuthCookies(context);
            return Results.Ok();
        }

        private static IResult Me(HttpContext context)
        {
            var subjectId = context.User.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)?.Value;
            var email = context.User.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.Email)?.Value;
            if (subjectId is null) return Results.Unauthorized();
            return Results.Ok(new AuthUserResponse(subjectId, email ?? string.Empty));
        }


    }
}

namespace Gateway.Api.Endpoints
{ 
    using System.Security.Claims;
    using System.Text.Json;
 
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

    using Gateway.Api.Tools;

    public static class DocumentEndpoints
    {
        private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new()
        {
            PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true
        };

        public static void MapDocumentEndpoints(this WebApplication app)
        {
            var group = app.MapGroup("/api/documents")
                .WithTags("Documents")
                .RequireAuthorization("mcp.read");

            group.MapGet("/search", Search)
                .WithName("SearchDocuments");
        }

        private static async Task<IResult> Search(
            [FromQuery] string keyword,
            [FromServices] GatewayTools tools,
            HttpContext context,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = context.User.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)?.Value;
            if (subjectId is null) return Results.Unauthorized();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(keyword))
                return Results.Ok(Array.Empty<DocumentSummaryResponse>());

            try
            {
                var json = await tools.SearchDocuments(subjectId, keyword, cancellationToken);
                var documents = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<DocumentSummaryResponse>>(json, JsonOptions);
                return Results.Ok(documents ?? []);
            }
            catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
            {
                return Results.Forbid();
            }
        }
    }

    public sealed record DocumentSummaryResponse(
        int Id,
        string Title,
        string ContentPreview);
}

namespace Gateway.Api.Endpoints
{  
    public static class GatewayEndpoints
    {
        private static readonly string[] DownstreamServices =
        [
            "taskservice",
        "documentservice"
        ];

        public static void MapGatewayEndpoints(this WebApplication app)
        {
            app.MapGet("/api/gateway/info", () => Results.Ok(new
            {
                service = "MCP Gateway",
                version = "1.0.0",
                timestamp = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
                downstream = DownstreamServices
            }))
            .AllowAnonymous()
            .WithTags("Gateway")
            .WithName("GatewayInfo");
        }
    }
}

namespace Gateway.Api.Endpoints
{
    using System.Security.Claims;
    using System.Text.Json;
 
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

    using Gateway.Api.Tools;

    public static class TaskEndpoints
    {
        private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new()
        {
            PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true
        };

        public static void MapTaskEndpoints(this WebApplication app)
        {
            var group = app.MapGroup("/api/tasks")
                .WithTags("Tasks")
                .RequireAuthorization("mcp.read");

            group.MapGet("/", ListTasks)
                .WithName("ListTasks");

            group.MapPost("/", CreateTask)
                .RequireAuthorization("mcp.write")
                .WithName("CreateTask");

            group.MapPatch("/{id}/complete", CompleteTask)
                .RequireAuthorization("mcp.write")
                .WithName("CompleteTask");
        }
         
        private static async Task<IResult> ListTasks(
            [FromServices] GatewayTools tools,
            HttpContext context,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectId(context);
            if (subjectId is null) return Results.Unauthorized();

            try
            {
                var json = await tools.ListTasks(subjectId, cancellationToken);
                var tasks = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<TaskItemResponse>>(json, JsonOptions);
                return Results.Ok(tasks ?? []);
            }
            catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
            {
                return Results.Forbid();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return Results.Problem($"ListTasks failed: {ex.Message}\n{ex.StackTrace}");
            }
        }

        private static async Task<IResult> CreateTask(
            CreateTaskRequest request,
            [FromServices] GatewayTools tools,
            HttpContext context,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectId(context);
            if (subjectId is null) return Results.Unauthorized();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Title))
                return Results.BadRequest(new { error = "Title is required" });

            try
            {
                var json = await tools.CreateTask(
                    subjectId, request.Title, request.Description ?? string.Empty, cancellationToken);
                var task = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<TaskItemResponse>(json, JsonOptions);
                return Results.Ok(task);
            }
            catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
            {
                return Results.Forbid();
            }
        }

        private static async Task<IResult> CompleteTask(
            int id,
            [FromServices] GatewayTools tools,
            HttpContext context,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectId(context);
            if (subjectId is null) return Results.Unauthorized();

            try
            {
                var json = await tools.CompleteTask(subjectId, id, cancellationToken);
                var success = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<bool>(json, JsonOptions);
                return Results.Ok(new { success });
            }
            catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
            {
                return Results.Forbid();
            }
        }

        private static string? GetSubjectId(HttpContext context)
        {
            return context.User.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)?.Value;
        }
    }

    public sealed record TaskItemResponse(
        int Id,
        string Title,
        string Description,
        bool IsComplete,
        DateTimeOffset CreatedAt);

    public sealed record CreateTaskRequest(string Title, string? Description);
}

namespace Gateway.Api.Infrastructure
{
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Antiforgery; 

    public sealed class CookieWriter(IAntiforgery antiforgery) : ICookieWriter
    {
        private const string AccessTokenCookie = "access_token";
        private const string CsrfCookie = "XSRF-TOKEN";

        public void WriteAuthCookies(HttpContext context, string accessToken)
        {
            context.Response.Cookies.Append(AccessTokenCookie, accessToken, new CookieOptions
            {
                HttpOnly = true,
                Secure = false,
                SameSite = SameSiteMode.Strict,
                Expires = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(15),
                Path = "/"
            });

            var tokens = antiforgery.GetAndStoreTokens(context);
            context.Response.Cookies.Append(CsrfCookie, tokens.RequestToken!, new CookieOptions
            {
                HttpOnly = false,
                Secure = false,
                SameSite = SameSiteMode.Strict,
                Path = "/"
            });
        }

        public void ClearAuthCookies(HttpContext context)
        {
            context.Response.Cookies.Delete(AccessTokenCookie, new CookieOptions { Path = "/" });
            context.Response.Cookies.Delete(CsrfCookie, new CookieOptions { Path = "/" });
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.DataProtection;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

using Gateway.Api.Configuration;
using Gateway.Api.Data;
using Gateway.Api.Endpoints;
using Gateway.Api.Infrastructure;
using Gateway.Api.Services;
using Gateway.Api.Tools;

using Gateway.Core.Abstractions;
using Gateway.Core.Entities;
using Gateway.Core.Policies;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.AddServiceDefaults();

builder.Services.AddDataProtection()
    .PersistKeysToFileSystem(new DirectoryInfo("/app/keys"))
    .SetApplicationName("mcp-gateway");

builder.Services.AddOptions<JwtOptions>()
    .Bind(builder.Configuration.GetSection(JwtOptions.SectionName))
    .ValidateDataAnnotations()
    .ValidateOnStart();

builder.Services.AddAuthentication(JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme)
    .AddJwtBearer();

builder.Services.ConfigureOptions<JwtBearerOptionsSetup>();
 
builder.Services.AddAuthorizationBuilder()
    .AddPolicy("mcp.read", policy => policy.RequireAssertion(context =>
        context.User.HasClaim(c =>
            c.Type == "scope" &&
            c.Value.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Contains("mcp.tools.read"))))
    .AddPolicy("mcp.write", policy => policy.RequireAssertion(context =>
        context.User.HasClaim(c =>
            c.Type == "scope" &&
            c.Value.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Contains("mcp.tools.write"))))
    .AddPolicy("mcp.admin", policy => policy.RequireAssertion(context =>
        context.User.HasClaim(c =>
            c.Type == "scope" &&
            c.Value.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Contains("mcp.tools.admin"))));

builder.Services.AddAntiforgery(options =>
{
    options.HeaderName = "X-XSRF-TOKEN";
    options.Cookie.Name = "XSRF-TOKEN";
    options.Cookie.HttpOnly = false;
    options.Cookie.SecurePolicy = CookieSecurePolicy.SameAsRequest;
    options.Cookie.SameSite = SameSiteMode.Strict;
});

builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
{
    options.AddPolicy("Client", policy =>
    {
        policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
            .AllowAnyHeader()
            .AllowAnyMethod()
            .AllowCredentials();
    });
});

builder.Services.AddSingleton<ITokenService, TokenService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IAuthService, AuthService>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<ICookieWriter, CookieWriter>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IToolPermissionPolicy, ScopeBasedToolPermissionPolicy>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IToolAuditLogger, InMemoryToolAuditLogger>();
builder.Services.AddHttpContextAccessor();

var taskServiceUrl = builder.Configuration["services:taskservice:http:0"]
    ?? "http://localhost:5001";
var documentServiceUrl = builder.Configuration["services:documentservice:http:0"]
    ?? "http://localhost:5002";

builder.Services.AddHttpClient("taskservice", client =>
    client.BaseAddress = new Uri(taskServiceUrl));
builder.Services.AddHttpClient("documentservice", client =>
    client.BaseAddress = new Uri(documentServiceUrl));

builder.Services.AddScoped<GatewayTools>();

builder.Services.AddMcpServer()
    .WithHttpTransport()
    .WithToolsFromAssembly();

builder.AddNpgsqlDbContext<UserDbContext>("userdb");
builder.Services.AddScoped<IPasswordHasher<User>, PasswordHasher<User>>();

var app = builder.Build();
 
using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
{
    var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<UserDbContext>();
    await db.Database.MigrateAsync();
}

app.UseCors("Client");
app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();
app.UseAntiforgery();

app.MapHealthChecks("/health");

app.MapAuthEndpoints();
app.MapTaskEndpoints();
app.MapDocumentEndpoints();
app.MapGatewayEndpoints();

app.MapMcp("/mcp").RequireAuthorization("mcp.read");

app.Run();

public partial class Program { }

namespace Gateway.Api.Services
{
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
    using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

    using Gateway.Api.Data;
    using Gateway.Core.Entities;
 
    public sealed class AuthService(
        UserDbContext db,
        ITokenService tokenService,
        IPasswordHasher<User> passwordHasher,
        ILogger<AuthService> logger) : IAuthService
    {
        private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, Exception?> _logUserRegistered =
            LoggerMessage.Define<string>(
                LogLevel.Information,
                new EventId(1, nameof(Register)),
                "User {Email} registered");

        private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, Exception?> _logUserLoggedIn =
            LoggerMessage.Define<string>(
                LogLevel.Information,
                new EventId(2, nameof(Login)),
                "User {Email} logged in");

        private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, Exception?> _logLoginFailedUserNotFound =
            LoggerMessage.Define<string>(
                LogLevel.Warning,
                new EventId(3, "LoginFailedUserNotFound"),
                "Login failed: user {Email} not found");

        private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, Exception?> _logLoginFailedWrongPassword =
            LoggerMessage.Define<string>(
                LogLevel.Warning,
                new EventId(4, "LoginFailedWrongPassword"),
                "Login failed: wrong password for {Email}");

        private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, Exception?> _logRegistrationFailedEmailExists =
            LoggerMessage.Define<string>(
                LogLevel.Warning,
                new EventId(5, "RegistrationFailedEmailExists"),
                "Registration failed: {Email} already exists");

        private static readonly Action<ILogger, string, Exception?> _logTokenGenerationFailed =
            LoggerMessage.Define<string>(
                LogLevel.Error,
                new EventId(6, "TokenGenerationFailed"),
                "Failed to generate token for {Email}");

        public AuthUserResponse? Register(RegisterRequest request)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Email) || request.Password.Length < 6)
                return null;

            if (db.Users.Any(u => u.Email == request.Email))
            {
                _logRegistrationFailedEmailExists(logger, request.Email, null);
                return null;
            }

            var user = new User
            {
                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
                Email = request.Email
            };
            user.PasswordHash = passwordHasher.HashPassword(user, request.Password);

            db.Users.Add(user);
            db.SaveChanges();

            _logUserRegistered(logger, request.Email, null);
            return new AuthUserResponse(user.Id.ToString(), user.Email);
        }

        public AuthUserResponse? Login(LoginRequest request)
        {
            var user = db.Users.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefault(u => u.Email == request.Email);
            if (user is null)
            {
                _logLoginFailedUserNotFound(logger, request.Email, null);
                return null;
            }

            var result = passwordHasher.VerifyHashedPassword(user, user.PasswordHash, request.Password);
            if (result == PasswordVerificationResult.Failed)
            {
                _logLoginFailedWrongPassword(logger, request.Email, null);
                return null;
            }

            _logUserLoggedIn(logger, request.Email, null);
            return new AuthUserResponse(user.Id.ToString(), user.Email);
        }

        //public string? GenerateTokenFor(AuthUserResponse user)
        //{
        //    try
        //    {
        //        return tokenService.CreateAccessToken(user.SubjectId, user.Email, "mcp.tools.read mcp.tools.write");
        //    }
        //    catch (Exception ex)
        //    {
        //        _logTokenGenerationFailed(logger, user.Email, ex);
        //        return null;
        //    }
        //}
        public string? GenerateTokenFor(AuthUserResponse user, params string[] scopes)
        {
            try
            {
                return tokenService.CreateAccessToken(user.SubjectId, user.Email, scopes);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _logTokenGenerationFailed(logger, user.Email, ex);
                return null;
            } 
        }
    }
}

####################################################################################################
namespace Gateway.Api.Services
{
    using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
    using System.Security.Claims;
    using System.Text;
 
    using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
    using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;

    using Gateway.Api.Configuration;
     
    public sealed class TokenService(IOptions<JwtOptions> options) : ITokenService
    {
        private readonly JwtOptions _options = options.Value;

        public string CreateAccessToken(string subjectId, string email, params string[] scopes)
        {
            var claims = new List<Claim>
        {
            new(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier, subjectId),
            new(ClaimTypes.Email, email),
            new("scope", string.Join(" ", scopes))
        };

            var credentials = new SigningCredentials(
                new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(_options.Key)),
                SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256);

            var token = new JwtSecurityToken(
                issuer: _options.Issuer,
                audience: _options.Audience,
                claims: claims,
                expires: DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(_options.AccessTokenMinutes),
                signingCredentials: credentials);

            return new JwtSecurityTokenHandler().WriteToken(token);
        }
    }
}

namespace Gateway.Api.Tools
{
    using System.Text.Json;
    using System.ComponentModel;
    using System.Diagnostics;
    using System.Security.Claims;
 
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;

    using ModelContextProtocol.Client;
    using ModelContextProtocol.Protocol;
    using ModelContextProtocol.Server;

    using Gateway.Core.Abstractions;
  
    [McpServerToolType]
    public sealed partial class GatewayTools
    {
        private readonly IHttpClientFactory _httpClientFactory;
        private readonly IToolPermissionPolicy _permissionPolicy;
        private readonly IToolAuditLogger _auditLogger;
        private readonly ILogger<GatewayTools> _logger;
        private readonly IHttpContextAccessor _httpContextAccessor;

        public GatewayTools(
            IHttpClientFactory httpClientFactory,
            IToolPermissionPolicy permissionPolicy,
            IToolAuditLogger auditLogger,
            ILogger<GatewayTools> logger,
            IHttpContextAccessor httpContextAccessor)
        {
            _httpClientFactory = httpClientFactory;
            _permissionPolicy = permissionPolicy;
            _auditLogger = auditLogger;
            _logger = logger;
            _httpContextAccessor = httpContextAccessor;
        }

        private string? GetSubjectIdFromContext()
        {
            return _httpContextAccessor.HttpContext?.User
                .FindFirst(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)?.Value;
        }

        [McpServerTool(Name = "list_tasks"), Description("Lists all tasks for the current user")]
        public async Task<string> ListTasksForAgent(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            var json = await ListTasks(subjectId, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                var tasks = doc.RootElement;

                if (tasks.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || tasks.GetArrayLength() == 0)
                    return "No tasks found.";

                var lines = new List<string>();
                foreach (var task in tasks.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var id = task.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();
                    var title = task.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    var isComplete = task.GetProperty("isComplete").GetBoolean();
                    lines.Add($"- [{id}] {title} - {(isComplete ? "Done" : "Open")}");
                }
                return $"You have {lines.Count} task(s):\n{string.Join("\n", lines)}";
            }
            catch
            {
                return json;
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> ListTasks(string subjectId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "list_tasks", "taskservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("list_tasks",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }
         
        [McpServerTool(Name = "create_task"), Description("Creates a new task. Title is required.")]
        public async Task<string> CreateTaskForAgent(
            [Description("Task title")] string title,
            [Description("Task description (optional)")] string description,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title))
                return "Error: Task title is required.";

            var json = await CreateTask(subjectId, title, description ?? string.Empty, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                var taskTitle = doc.RootElement.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                return $"Task created: \"{taskTitle}\"";
            }
            catch
            {
                return json;
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> CreateTask(
            string subjectId, string title, string? description,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "create_task", "taskservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("create_task",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?>
                    {
                        ["subjectId"] = subjectId,
                        ["title"] = title,
                        ["description"] = description ?? string.Empty
                    },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }
         
        [McpServerTool(Name = "complete_task"), Description("Marks a task as complete by its title")]
        public async Task<string> CompleteTaskForAgent(
            [Description("Task title to mark as complete")] string title,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            var listJson = await ListTasks(subjectId, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(listJson);
                var tasks = doc.RootElement;

                if (tasks.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array)
                    return "Error: Could not read tasks.";

                int? taskId = null;
                foreach (var task in tasks.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var taskTitle = task.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    if (string.Equals(taskTitle, title, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        taskId = task.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (taskId is null)
                    return $"No task found with title \"{title}\".";

                var json = await CompleteTask(subjectId, taskId.Value, cancellationToken);
                var success = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<bool>(json);
                return success
                    ? $"Task \"{title}\" marked as Done."
                    : $"Could not mark task \"{title}\" as Done.";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return $"Error: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> CompleteTask(
            string subjectId, int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "complete_task", "taskservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("complete_task",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId, ["id"] = id },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }
         
        [McpServerTool(Name = "get_task"), Description("Gets a task by its title")]
        public async Task<string> GetTaskForAgent(
            [Description("Task title")] string title,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            var listJson = await ListTasks(subjectId, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(listJson);
                var tasks = doc.RootElement;

                foreach (var task in tasks.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var taskTitle = task.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    if (string.Equals(taskTitle, title, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        var isComplete = task.GetProperty("isComplete").GetBoolean();
                        var description = task.TryGetProperty("description", out var desc) ? desc.GetString() : "";
                        return $"Task: \"{title}\"\nStatus: {(isComplete ? "Done" : "Open")}\nDescription: {description}";
                    }
                }

                return $"No task found with title \"{title}\".";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return $"Error: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> GetTask(
            string subjectId, int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "get_task", "taskservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("get_task",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId, ["id"] = id },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        [McpServerTool(Name = "search_tasks"), Description("Searches tasks by title keyword. Keyword is required.")]
        public async Task<string> SearchTasksForAgent(
            [Description("Keyword to search in task titles. Must not be empty.")] string keyword,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(keyword))
                return "Error: keyword is required. Use list_tasks to see all tasks.";

            var json = await SearchTasks(subjectId, keyword, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = System.Text.Json.JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                var tasks = doc.RootElement;

                if (tasks.ValueKind != System.Text.Json.JsonValueKind.Array || tasks.GetArrayLength() == 0)
                    return $"No tasks found matching \"{keyword}\".";

                var lines = new List<string>();
                foreach (var task in tasks.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var title = task.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    var isComplete = task.GetProperty("isComplete").GetBoolean();
                    lines.Add($"- {title} — {(isComplete ? "Done" : "Open")}");
                }
                return $"Found {lines.Count} task(s) matching \"{keyword}\":\n{string.Join("\n", lines)}";
            }
            catch
            {
                return json;
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> SearchTasks(
            string subjectId, string keyword, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "search_tasks", "taskservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("search_tasks",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId, ["keyword"] = keyword },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }
         
        [McpServerTool(Name = "delete_task"), Description("Deletes a task by its title")]
        public async Task<string> DeleteTaskForAgent(
            [Description("Task title to delete")] string title,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            var listJson = await ListTasks(subjectId, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(listJson);
                var tasks = doc.RootElement;

                if (tasks.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array)
                    return "Error: Could not read tasks.";

                int? taskId = null;
                foreach (var task in tasks.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var taskTitle = task.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    if (string.Equals(taskTitle, title, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        taskId = task.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (taskId is null)
                    return $"No task found with title \"{title}\".";

                var json = await DeleteTask(subjectId, taskId.Value, cancellationToken);
                var success = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<bool>(json);
                return success
                    ? $"Task \"{title}\" deleted."
                    : $"Could not delete task \"{title}\".";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return $"Error: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> DeleteTask(
            string subjectId, int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "delete_task", "taskservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("delete_task",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId, ["id"] = id },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        [McpServerTool(Name = "search_documents"), Description("Searches documents by keyword. Keyword is required.")]
        public async Task<string> SearchDocumentsForAgent(
            [Description("Keyword")] string keyword,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(keyword))
                return "Error: keyword is required. Use a specific search term.";

            var json = await SearchDocuments(subjectId, keyword, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                var documents = doc.RootElement;

                if (documents.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || documents.GetArrayLength() == 0)
                    return $"No documents found matching \"{keyword}\".";

                var lines = new List<string>();
                foreach (var document in documents.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var id = document.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();
                    var title = document.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    lines.Add($"- [{id}] {title}");
                }
                return $"Found {lines.Count} document(s) matching \"{keyword}\":\n{string.Join("\n", lines)}";
            }
            catch
            {
                return json;
            }
        }

        [McpServerTool(Name = "list_documents"), Description("Lists all documents in the knowledge base")]
        public async Task<string> ListDocumentsForAgent(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            var json = await ListDocuments(subjectId, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                var documents = doc.RootElement;

                if (documents.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || documents.GetArrayLength() == 0)
                    return "No documents found.";

                var lines = new List<string>();
                foreach (var document in documents.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var title = document.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    var id = document.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();
                    lines.Add($"- [{id}] {title}");
                }
                return $"You have {lines.Count} document(s):\n{string.Join("\n", lines)}";
            }
            catch
            {
                return json;
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> ListDocuments(string subjectId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "list_documents", "documentservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("list_documents",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        public async Task<string> SearchDocuments(
            string subjectId, string keyword, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "search_documents", "documentservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("search_documents",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId, ["keyword"] = keyword },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        [McpServerTool(Name = "delete_document"), Description("Deletes a document by its title")]
        public async Task<string> DeleteDocumentForAgent(
            [Description("Document title to delete")] string title,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title))
                return "Error: Document title is required.";

            var searchJson = await SearchDocuments(subjectId, title, cancellationToken);

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(searchJson);
                var documents = doc.RootElement;

                if (documents.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || documents.GetArrayLength() == 0)
                    return $"No document found with title \"{title}\".";

                var firstDoc = documents[0];
                var docId = firstDoc.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();
                var docTitle = firstDoc.GetProperty("title").GetString();

                var json = await DeleteDocument(subjectId, docId, cancellationToken);
                var success = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<bool>(json);
                return success
                    ? $"Document \"{docTitle}\" deleted."
                    : $"Could not delete document \"{docTitle}\".";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return $"Error: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> DeleteDocument(
            string subjectId, int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "delete_document", "documentservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("delete_document",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId, ["id"] = id },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        //    }
        //}
        [McpServerTool(Name = "get_document"), Description("Gets a specific document by its title")]
        public async Task<string> GetDocumentForAgent(
    [Description("Document title")] string title,
    CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[GATEWAY] get_document called with title: '{title}'");

            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title))
                return "Error: Document title is required.";

            var searchJson = await SearchDocuments(subjectId, title, cancellationToken);
            Console.WriteLine($"[GATEWAY] search results: {searchJson}");

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(searchJson);
                var documents = doc.RootElement;

                if (documents.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array && documents.GetArrayLength() > 0)
                {
                    var firstDoc = documents[0];
                    var docId = firstDoc.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();

                    var json = await GetDocument(subjectId, docId, cancellationToken);

                    using var fullDoc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                    var fullTitle = fullDoc.RootElement.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                    var content = fullDoc.RootElement.TryGetProperty("content", out var c) ? c.GetString() : "";
                    return $"Document: \"{fullTitle}\"\nContent: {content}";
                }

                var allJson = await ListDocuments(subjectId, cancellationToken);
                Console.WriteLine($"[GATEWAY] list_documents results: {allJson}");

                using var allDoc = JsonDocument.Parse(allJson);
                var allDocuments = allDoc.RootElement;

                if (allDocuments.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array)
                {
                    foreach (var d in allDocuments.EnumerateArray())
                    {
                        var docTitle = d.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                        if (string.Equals(docTitle, title, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            var id = d.GetProperty("id").GetInt32();
                            var fullJson = await GetDocument(subjectId, id, cancellationToken);

                            using var fullDoc = JsonDocument.Parse(fullJson);
                            var fullTitle = fullDoc.RootElement.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                            var content = fullDoc.RootElement.TryGetProperty("content", out var c) ? c.GetString() : "";
                            return $"Document: \"{fullTitle}\"\nContent: {content}";
                        }
                    }
                }

                return $"No document found with title \"{title}\".";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return $"Error: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> GetDocument(
            string subjectId, int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "get_document", "documentservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("get_document",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["subjectId"] = subjectId, ["id"] = id },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        [McpServerTool(Name = "add_document"), Description("Adds a new document to the knowledge base")]
        public async Task<string> AddDocumentForAgent(

            [Description("Document content")] string content,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);

            }
        }

            string subjectId, string title, string content,

        [McpServerTool(Name = "update_document"), Description("Updates an existing document by its ID")]
        public async Task<string> UpdateDocumentForAgent(
            [Description("Document ID")] int id,
            [Description("New document title")] string title,
            [Description("New document content")] string content,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var subjectId = GetSubjectIdFromContext();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(subjectId))
                return "Error: No subject ID found in token.";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title))
                return "Error: Document title is required.";

            var json = await UpdateDocument(subjectId, id, title, content, cancellationToken);

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(json) || json == "null")
                return $"No document found with id {id}.";

            try
            {
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                var docTitle = doc.RootElement.GetProperty("title").GetString();
                return $"Document with ID {id} updated: \"{docTitle}\"";
            }
            catch
            {
                return json;
            }
        }

        public async Task<string> UpdateDocument(
            string subjectId, int id, string title, string content,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            return await InvokeDownstreamAsync(
                "update_document", "documentservice", subjectId,
                async client => await client.CallToolAsync("update_document",
                    new Dictionary<string, object?>
                    {
                        ["subjectId"] = subjectId,
                        ["id"] = id,
                        ["title"] = title,
                        ["content"] = content
                    },
                    cancellationToken: cancellationToken),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        private async Task<string> InvokeDownstreamAsync(
            string toolName, string serviceName, string subjectId,
            Func<McpClient, Task<CallToolResult>> invoke,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var allowed = await _permissionPolicy.IsAllowedAsync(subjectId, toolName, serviceName, cancellationToken);
            if (!allowed)
            {
                await _auditLogger.LogAsync(
                    new ToolInvocationRecord(subjectId, toolName, serviceName, "denied",
                        DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, 0), cancellationToken);
                throw new UnauthorizedAccessException($"Subject {subjectId} not allowed to call {toolName}");
            }

            var httpClient = _httpClientFactory.CreateClient(serviceName);
            var transport = new HttpClientTransport(new HttpClientTransportOptions
            {
                Endpoint = new Uri(httpClient.BaseAddress!, "/mcp"),
                Name = serviceName
            });

            await using var client = await McpClient.CreateAsync(transport, cancellationToken: cancellationToken);

            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            try
            {
                var result = await invoke(client);
                sw.Stop();
                await _auditLogger.LogAsync(
                    new ToolInvocationRecord(subjectId, toolName, serviceName, "success",
                        DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds), cancellationToken);

                return result.Content.OfType<TextContentBlock>().FirstOrDefault()?.Text ?? string.Empty;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                sw.Stop();
                LogDownstreamToolFailed(_logger, toolName, serviceName, ex);
                await _auditLogger.LogAsync(
                    new ToolInvocationRecord(subjectId, toolName, serviceName, "error",
                        DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds), cancellationToken);
                throw;
            }
        }

        [LoggerMessage(Level = LogLevel.Error, Message = "Downstream tool {Tool} failed on {Service}")]
        private static partial void LogDownstreamToolFailed(ILogger logger, string tool, string service, Exception ex);
    }

    public sealed class InMemoryToolAuditLogger : IToolAuditLogger
    {
        private readonly List<ToolInvocationRecord> _records = [];

        public Task LogAsync(ToolInvocationRecord record, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            _records.Add(record);
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        }
    }
}

namespace Gateway.Core.Policies
{
    using Gateway.Core.Abstractions;

    //public sealed class ScopeBasedToolPermissionPolicy : IToolPermissionPolicy
    //{
    //    private static readonly Dictionary<string, HashSet<string>> ScopeToolMap = new()
    //    {
    //        ["mcp.tools.read"] = ["list_tasks", "get_task", "search_tasks", "search_documents", "get_document"],
    //        ["mcp.tools.write"] = ["create_task", "complete_task", "add_document"],
    //        ["mcp.tools.admin"] = ["delete_task", "delete_document"]
    //    };

    //    public Task<bool> IsAllowedAsync(
    //        string subjectId,
    //        string toolName,
    //        string downstreamService,
    //        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    //    {
    //        var allowed = ScopeToolMap.Values.Any(set => set.Contains(toolName));
    //        return Task.FromResult(allowed);
    //    }
    //}
    public sealed class ScopeBasedToolPermissionPolicy : IToolPermissionPolicy
    {
        private static readonly Dictionary<string, HashSet<string>> ScopeToolMap = new()
        {
            ["mcp.tools.read"] =
                ["list_tasks", "get_task", "search_tasks", "list_documents", "search_documents", "get_document"],
            ["mcp.tools.write"] = ["create_task", "complete_task", "add_document", "update_document"],
            ["mcp.tools.admin"] = ["delete_task", "delete_document"]
        };

        public Task<bool> IsAllowedAsync(
            string subjectId,
            string toolName,
            string downstreamService,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            var allowed = ScopeToolMap.Values.Any(set => set.Contains(toolName));
            return Task.FromResult(allowed);
        }
    }
}

namespace Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting
{
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Builder;
    using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
    using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Diagnostics.HealthChecks;
    using Microsoft.Extensions.Diagnostics.HealthChecks;
    using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging; 

    using OpenTelemetry;
    using OpenTelemetry.Metrics;
    using OpenTelemetry.Trace; 

    public static class Extensions
    {
        private const string HealthEndpointPath = "/health";
        private const string AlivenessEndpointPath = "/alive";

        public static TBuilder AddServiceDefaults<TBuilder>(this TBuilder builder) where TBuilder : IHostApplicationBuilder
        {
            builder.ConfigureOpenTelemetry();
            builder.AddDefaultHealthChecks();
            builder.Services.AddServiceDiscovery();

            builder.Services.ConfigureHttpClientDefaults(http =>
            {
                http.AddStandardResilienceHandler(options =>
                {
                    options.Retry.MaxRetryAttempts = 3;
                    options.Retry.UseJitter = true;
                    options.CircuitBreaker.SamplingDuration = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30);
                    options.AttemptTimeout.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10);
                });
                http.AddServiceDiscovery();
            });

            return builder;
        }

        public static TBuilder ConfigureOpenTelemetry<TBuilder>(this TBuilder builder) where TBuilder : IHostApplicationBuilder
        {
            builder.Logging.AddOpenTelemetry(logging =>
            {
                logging.IncludeFormattedMessage = true;
                logging.IncludeScopes = true;
            });

            builder.Services.AddOpenTelemetry()
                .WithMetrics(metrics =>
                {
                    metrics.AddAspNetCoreInstrumentation()
                        .AddHttpClientInstrumentation()
                        .AddRuntimeInstrumentation();
                })
                .WithTracing(tracing =>
                {
                    tracing.AddSource(builder.Environment.ApplicationName)
                        .AddAspNetCoreInstrumentation(tracing =>
                            tracing.Filter = context =>
                                !context.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments(HealthEndpointPath)
                                && !context.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments(AlivenessEndpointPath)
                        )
                        .AddHttpClientInstrumentation();
                });

            builder.AddOpenTelemetryExporters();
            return builder;
        }

        private static TBuilder AddOpenTelemetryExporters<TBuilder>(this TBuilder builder) where TBuilder : IHostApplicationBuilder
        {
            var useOtlpExporter = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(builder.Configuration["OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT"]);
            if (useOtlpExporter)
            {
                builder.Services.AddOpenTelemetry().UseOtlpExporter();
            }
            return builder;
        }

        public static TBuilder AddDefaultHealthChecks<TBuilder>(this TBuilder builder) where TBuilder : IHostApplicationBuilder
        {
            builder.Services.AddHealthChecks()
                .AddCheck("self", () => HealthCheckResult.Healthy(), ["live"]);
            return builder;
        }

        public static WebApplication MapDefaultEndpoints(this WebApplication app)
        {
            if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
            {
                app.MapHealthChecks(HealthEndpointPath);
                app.MapHealthChecks(AlivenessEndpointPath, new HealthCheckOptions
                {
                    Predicate = r => r.Tags.Contains("live")
                });
            }
            return app;
        }
    }
}

# SIZE : 1524 B
# EXT  : .cs
####################################################################################################
namespace DocumentService.Api.Data
{
    using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

    public sealed class DocumentDbContext(DbContextOptions<DocumentDbContext> options) : DbContext(options)
    {
        public DbSet<Document> Documents => Set<Document>();

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            modelBuilder.HasPostgresExtension("vector");
            modelBuilder.HasPostgresExtension("pg_trgm");

            modelBuilder.Entity<Document>(entity =>
            {
                entity.HasKey(e => e.Id);
                entity.Property(e => e.Title).HasMaxLength(300).IsRequired();
                entity.Property(e => e.Content).IsRequired();
                entity.Property(e => e.Embedding).HasColumnType("vector(768)");

                entity.HasIndex(e => e.CreatedAt);

                entity.HasIndex(e => e.Title)
                    .HasMethod("gin")
                    .HasOperators("gin_trgm_ops");

                entity.HasIndex(e => e.Content)
                    .HasMethod("gin")
                    .HasOperators("gin_trgm_ops");
            });
        }
    }

    public sealed class Document
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public string Title { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string Content { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public Pgvector.Vector? Embedding { get; set; }
        public DateTimeOffset CreatedAt { get; set; } = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
    }
}

FROM mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/aspnet:10.0 AS base
WORKDIR /app
EXPOSE 8080

FROM mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/sdk:10.0 AS build
ARG BUILD_CONFIGURATION=Release
WORKDIR /src
COPY ["src/Services/DocumentService.Api/DocumentService.Api.csproj", "src/Services/DocumentService.Api/"]
COPY ["src/ServiceDefaults/ServiceDefaults.csproj", "src/ServiceDefaults/"]
COPY ["Directory.Build.props", "./"]
COPY ["Directory.Packages.props", "./"]
COPY ["global.json", "./"]
RUN dotnet restore "./src/Services/DocumentService.Api/DocumentService.Api.csproj"
COPY . .
WORKDIR "/src/src/Services/DocumentService.Api"
RUN dotnet build "./DocumentService.Api.csproj" -c $BUILD_CONFIGURATION -o /app/build

FROM build AS publish
ARG BUILD_CONFIGURATION=Release
RUN dotnet publish "./DocumentService.Api.csproj" -c $BUILD_CONFIGURATION -o /app/publish /p:UseAppHost=false

FROM base AS final
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=publish /app/publish .
ENTRYPOINT ["dotnet", "DocumentService.Api.dll"]

# FILE 79 / 146
# PATH : src\Services\DocumentService.Api\Program.cs
# SIZE : 723 B
# EXT  : .cs
####################################################################################################
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

using DocumentService.Api.Data;
using DocumentService.Api.Tools;
 
var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.AddServiceDefaults();
builder.AddNpgsqlDbContext<DocumentDbContext>("documentdb", configureDbContextOptions: options =>
    options.UseNpgsql(npgsql => npgsql.UseVector()));

builder.Services.AddMcpServer()
    .WithHttpTransport()
    .WithTools<DocumentTools>();

var app = builder.Build();
 
using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
{
    var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<DocumentDbContext>();
    await db.Database.MigrateAsync();
}

app.MapHealthChecks("/health");
app.MapMcp("/mcp");

app.Run();

public partial class Program { }
